## Direction

- **Push the paper's evaluation section** (`IB/evaluation_section_snapshot.tex`) to an MVP (user, Oct 13). Gently warn against 1–2 % micro-optimizing.
- **Compaction:** the results are accepted as the MVP (user, Oct 14 ≈09:15Z, again 13:06Z: "just good enough for our MVP, but more is better"). Hill-climbing continues **on the side**, at 1/10 only until it reaches a good point (then all three sizes).
- **Compaction on 9B-written prefixes:** medium-high priority, never interrupting other work (user, ≈13:58Z). Reason: "explaining that the prefix is from 27B, but the continuation from 9B just makes no sense". Rerun the baselines and AC on 9B prefixes; if AC is bad there, queue AC training on 9B prefixes.
- **Trajectory Q/A:** green light (user, 13:14Z). It takes the EC2 nodes first and should use multi-GPU, especially 2× GH200/H200 (user, ≈13:45Z). Prepare the last-mile synthesis SFT in parallel when GPUs allow.
- **40k rule:** trajectory Q/A and agent-collaboration runs stop at 40k tokens, compaction off (root canon).

## Rules (in force)

- **Autonomy (user, 2026-10-19; full text in ROOT_CANON "Autonomy: do not block on the user"):** never wait for the user except in extreme circumstances. Main-source code changes, cleanups, hyperparameter tweaks, hill-climbing, new arms and GPU reallocation among our own runs need no approval. Print the main result tables in chat so the user can skim. Hard limits (credentials, guarded cluster access, off-limits instance/reservation, no optimizer.pt copies, no TASK.md, commit only when asked) still hold.
- **Current top priority (2026-10-19):** AM_COMPACTION_PLAN (Qwen3-4B LongHealth, 1/10): step 1 AM cache through our handler, then 2a KL from the single-patient teacher. EC2 nodes (now cqa-ec2r + cqa-ec2b) belong to it. **User (2026-10-19 ≈23:05Z): "I would not automatically shutdown EC2 until you can see clearly that csail is back and working."** → no automatic EC2 teardown at 05:30Z (keepalives extended to 18:00Z); tear down only after CSAIL is verified working (guarded call ok, squeue ok, a job runs) and EC2 results are on S3.
- **Check-ins** every 30 min as background timers (`cd /workspace; sleep 1740; date -u +%H:%MZ; echo "30-minute check-in due"`). Read nodes, container df, plan interactions mtime, CSAIL squeue/df. Log here with times read from `date -u`. Never state numbers that weren't read; verify a submitted job started before reporting it as running.
- **GPUs:**
  - EC2: ≤2 nodes × 2 GPUs (pc-a, pc-b), all Sky/EC2 commands under `export SSH_AUTH_SOCK=` with `-o IdentityAgent=none`. Never touch i-0918b8f73e9a0fe03.
  - CSAIL: never more than two GH200 jobs; group H200 at most two GPUs. ORCD reservation 23493337 off limits.
- **Cluster access:**
  - ORCD/CSAIL only via `IB/TMP/AGENT_AC_PRETRAINING/ac_recon/{orcd,csail}_ssh.sh`, run from /workspace. Exit 97 means the user runs `! ssh csail echo ok`; never start Duo/auth.
  - rsync to csail with `-e "ssh -o BatchMode=yes -o ControlMaster=no -o ProxyCommand=false -o ProxyJump=none"`.
  - Unattended loops: one call per cycle, ≥50 min apart on CSAIL.
  - Nothing heavy on login nodes.
- **CSAIL files:** never replace existing files (new folders, `--ignore-existing`, `cp -n`). No `sbatch --export=ALL,...` (pass variables through the environment). Never edit a run folder's recipe.json. Never copy optimizer.pt. Make a pair from `AC_MODELS/rag_poc/latest/{ac_modules.pt,side_lora}` + `LORAS/rag_reader/latest/{adapter_model.safetensors,adapter_config.json}`, then delete the optimizer state and read snapshots.
- **Secrets and repo:** `.env` and tokens never leave the container. Do not read or edit TASK.md. No reset. Commit only when asked; commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Training defaults:** rates 2e-5 / 2e-5 (never 5e-5; no raise without the user's word); per-layer additions only with the user's word.
- **Shell and process hygiene:**
  - `rm -rf` only with literal paths.
  - Never edit a script while a background instance of it runs.
  - Never pgrep a pattern that also matches the waiting command itself (happened twice on Oct 14).
  - Start every command with `cd /workspace`.
- **Subagents:** review/core subagents use Opus; batch judges use Sonnet.
- **Tressoir:** plans validated with `node IB/skills/tressoir-artifact-md/scripts/check_md.js <file>`; `touch` the projection after a PLAN.md change.
- **Working without the user** (Oct 6 rules, still in force; full text in the archive): never block on the user; free disk without asking above 90 % (oldest unflagged first, inventory line here); take another node at once if one dies; detach anything that must survive (nohup/sbatch); kill only my own jobs, by id; pick the option that gives a number sooner; own misses in one sentence.

### 2026-10-27T15:11:47Z Select-then-answer text baseline (user, Oct 27, high priority; an AI reviewer's objection)
- The decode twin of the AC encode path. The same Qwen3.5-2B reads the question first, then each call of 20 BM25 passages, and DECODES (greedy, thinking off) instead of writing ACs. The 9B then reads that text (thinking off, 3 passes = the Fig. 3 text settings).
  - sel{K}: per call, at most 2 passages named (2/20 = the AC ratio 1/10); the selected passages are given verbatim.
  - sum{K}: per call, a note of at most 240 tokens (the AC budget of a call).
